## assistant

The plan needs revision before implementation. It still specifies the superseded release sequence, and several database and interaction contracts are incomplete.

1. **P1 — The build order contradicts the settled decisions.** [PLAN.md:96](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:96) ships layout separately from insights. The [capture:135](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-visual-upgrade.md:135) explicitly supersedes PR #133 and combines them; [capture:154](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-visual-upgrade.md:154) also requires the finding-to-resource registry and evidence-attached Ask draft, neither of which appears in the plan.
   **Fix:** Replace the release sequence with the settled v3 split and explicitly enumerate the registry, Ask draft, and subsequent session-brief/review/Today work.

2. **P1 — The proposed message uniqueness constraint cannot be created as specified.** [PLAN.md:80](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:80) requires `(recipient, event_key)` on both tables, but messages contain `conversation_id` and `sender_id`, with recipients represented through participants ([schema:24](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260416234013_49bfd508-03f2-4942-a636-2763f1f3af5b.sql:24)). Also, a unique index makes duplicate inserts fail; it does not make retries successful no-ops.
   **Fix:** Define actual per-table identity columns and explicit conflict handling, including recovery when only one of the two writes succeeds.

3. **P1 — Update immutability does not protect event identity at insertion.** [PLAN.md:81](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:81) protects changes to `origin` and `event_key`, but the existing message INSERT policy permits participant-authored messages without restricting those new fields ([schema:110](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260416234013_49bfd508-03f2-4942-a636-2763f1f3af5b.sql:110)). A student could supply a system origin and colliding event key.
   **Fix:** Enforce ordinary-user inserts as `origin='human' AND event_key IS NULL`, reserve generated identities for authorized writers, and test forged inserts.

4. **P1 — Legacy generated events have no defined read transition.** [PLAN.md:82](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:82) includes legacy rows with null keys, while the following paragraph acknowledges generated events exclusively by key. Those events cannot follow the specified acknowledgement path; legacy messages also lack a defined human/system classification.
   **Fix:** Give legacy feed events stable synthetic identities and explicit classification/read rules without rewriting historical rows.

5. **P1 — The two unread systems will disagree.** [PLAN.md:79](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:79) leaves `useInbox` untouched, but its unread calculation ignores acknowledgements and uses the latest message ([useInbox.ts:192](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/hooks/useInbox.ts:192)). Opening a generated event can clear the Home bell while leaving the existing inbox unread; opening its conversation updates only the watermark, leaving the new event acknowledgement absent.
   **Fix:** Specify consistent read semantics across Home, the bell, and conversations, and adapt existing unread consumers through a shared selector.

6. **P1 — Rescheduled reminders reuse their old identity.** [PLAN.md:92](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:92) promises re-keying on reschedule but defines the key only from booking ID and offset. Rescheduling the same booking after its first reminder makes the replacement collide with the old event or inherit its acknowledgement.
   **Fix:** Include a booking revision or scheduled-start timestamp in the key and revalidate that revision immediately before delivery.

7. **P1 — Fresh-practice actions ship before their dependency, and the named queue-building location is wrong.** [PLAN.md:72](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:72) requires “Try new ones” for phase-two insights, while the filter extension sits with the later By-type work. [drillRunSpec.ts:146](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/lib/drillRunSpec.ts:146) merely constructs configuration; actual bank filtering occurs in [Drill.tsx:565](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/pages/Drill.tsx:565).
   **Fix:** Ship exposure exclusion and persisted-queue resolution before the insight CTA, applying exclusion before sampling and preserving that queue on resume.

8. **P1 — The new RPCs lack an authorization contract.** [PLAN.md:56](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:56) accepts caller-supplied class IDs and returns question-level evidence, but specifies neither execution security nor cross-student tests; the feed RPC is similarly unspecified. Existing aggregate RPCs deliberately use `SECURITY INVOKER` ([migration:89](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260709235900_phase6_query_rpcs.sql:89)).
   **Fix:** Require invoker/RLS enforcement or explicit owner/admin authorization, pinned search paths, bounded inputs, and anonymous/cross-student denial tests for every new RPC.

9. **P2 — The design’s comparative statistics cannot come from the proposed response.** [Home3.dc.html:108](C:/Users/Joshu/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/fe553725-d881-4068-8809-1fb7b342eee9/scratchpad/artifact-files/307bbd6c-86e0-43cd-a5df-8735d306b8fb/project/Home3.dc.html:108) shows change against the previous 30 days; [PLAN.md:56](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:56) returns one window. Requesting 60 days does not produce two comparable windows because latest-attempt deduplication can select different attempts.
   **Fix:** Return separately defined current/prior buckets with a shared cutoff and sample floors, and hide comparisons lacking sufficient evidence.

10. **P2 — The visual contract omits truthful bubble sizing and accessible labels.** [Home3.dc.html:73](C:/Users/Joshu/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/fe553725-d881-4068-8809-1fb7b342eee9/scratchpad/artifact-files/307bbd6c-86e0-43cd-a5df-8735d306b8fb/project/Home3.dc.html:73) claims circles encode missed questions, but their diameters are hardcoded. The primary bubble also uses white labels over orange; white against `#ff8906` is approximately **2.38:1**. Keyboard testing alone will not catch that.
    **Fix:** Specify area-proportional sizing, readable text independent of bubble size, a textual equivalent, and contrast checks across gradients and themes.

11. **P2 — Empty, failed, and unavailable data remain conflated.** [PLAN.md:66](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:66) offers only eligible evidence or `null`, then converts zero eligible lenses into “Start here.” A failed reader can therefore masquerade as no practice, and a failed pinned source can become “pinned view has no data,” contradicting [capture:153](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-visual-upgrade.md:153).
    **Fix:** Define loading/error/empty/insufficient/ready adapter states and prohibit failure or pending data from producing empty-state claims.

12. **P2 — RC history reuse risks truncated results and unnecessary downloads.** [PLAN.md:59](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:59) points to `rcProgress.ts`, whose `getQuestionHistory()` requests all attempts without pagination and whose `listSessions()` requests all sessions. Parallelizing these reads does not make them bounded or guarantee complete results.
    **Fix:** Add a bounded Home aggregate that explicitly handles first attempts, excluded sessions, completion, and recency; fetch detailed evidence only when opened.

13. **P2 — The RC launcher duplicates an existing section launch path.** [PLAN.md:46](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:46) proposes new session creation, but [rcSections.ts:131](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/features/rc/lib/rcSections.ts:131) already launches complete sections with accommodations, untimed mode, and end-of-set reveal. PR #133 also does not supply the promised Section tab: at branch commit `8b4c406`, `ClassroomHomeView.tsx:287` only opens the existing finder.
    **Fix:** Reuse the existing RC section contract, extend custom timing where necessary, and explicitly build/test Section-tab selection rather than treating PR #133 as that dependency.

Read-only review; no files modified. Findings are based on the supplied documents and source inspection, not runtime or deployed-database verification.

VERDICT: REVISE

Home = `/classroom` in the Classroom frame (glossary `src/components/classroom/CONTEXT.md`).\\r\\n8:- Home code: `src/components/classroom/home/`.\\r\\n9:- PR #133 (branch `feat/home-v2-phase1`, unmerged): `pickContinueWork`, `QuestionFinder` `open()` handle, container-query columns, preservation tests. v3 builds on it and closes #133. #133 has NO Section tab; \\"Timed section\\" only opens the finder.\\r\\n10:- Hard rules: completed student work never lost or modified; additive migrations only; branch + PR; Lovable Cloud Edge Functions deploy through Lovable (confirm first); frontend reaches lsatprep.study only via Lovable deploy; a teacher is never the student; student copy per `wiki/teaching-voice.md`, no em dashes.\\r\\n11:- Builder: Codex `gpt-6-sol`, low. Spec/review: Claude + `gpt-6-astra`.\\r\\n12:\\r\\n13:## Goal\\r\\n14:Ship the v3 Classroom Home for students: a warm top panel (greeting, two numbers, Question finder, Continue, Smart Drill, Timed section, Inbox), a dominant visual insight that tells the student what to do next, a precise per-type list, supporting tiles, and the unchanged Next seven days strip. Every number says what it counts; the page is fast, stable, accessible, and complete with zero coach input.\\r\\n15:\\r\\n16:## Approach\\r\\n17:\\r\\n18:### 0. Preservation checklist (every PR)\\r\\n19:Assigned work rows and actions; timed-section briefing interception; report-card seen tracking; message preview dismissal watermark; `HomeAccess` gates; Question finder never gated; coach account never offered assigned work; Next seven days strip markup, classes and CSS unchanged; today\'s full Classroom menu unchanged (v3\'s slim rail is NOT built).\\r\\n20:\\r\\n21:### 1. Layout\\r\\n22:1. **Top panel** (full width, warm gradient): date line, greeting, status line, two numbers: questions this week (`daily_stats.questions_answered`, last 7 local days) and days to test day (`profiles.test_date`; hidden when null). Right: Question finder bar; dark Continue card (earliest-due eligible assigned work; hidden when none); Smart Drill (`kind: \'smart-drill\'`); Timed section (opens the finder, as #133); Inbox with the existing unread count to `/inbox`.\\r\\n23:2. **Main row**: main insight (8/12, taupe) + Next session (4/12, dark). No booking: compact \\"Book a session\\" (existing CTA), never hidden or an empty shell.\\r\\n24:3. **Lower row**: per-type list (7/12) + tiles (5/12): Last section; From Joshua (existing latest-message preview); Wrong answers to revisit (journal items due this week).\\r\\n25:4. **Next seven days**: unchanged.\\r\\n26:Columns by container width (existing container query); one column under 900px container; phone order: top panel, Continue, main insight, Next session, list, tiles, week.\\r\\n27:\\r\\n28:### 2. Data layer\\r\\n29:**Adapter states.** Every adapter returns one of `loading | error | empty | insufficient | ready`. Only `empty` (confirmed zero rows) may produce \\"start here\\" copy; `error` shows \\"Could not load this, retry\\" with the last good value and its date if cached; `insufficient` shows the labelled single-result fallback. The engine never turns `error` or `loading` into an empty claim.\\r\\n30:\\r\\n31:**New RPCs** (additive migration), all `security invoker`, `set search_path = public`, matching `20260709235900_phase6_query_rpcs.sql:25`, so existing RLS on `attempts` / `section_history` decides access; `p_days` bounded 7..90; tests: anonymous denied, student A cannot read student B, admin can read any.\\r\\n32:- `home_lr_type_accuracy(p_class_id, p_days int default 30)`: two buckets with one shared cutoff: `current` = (now - p_days, now], `prior` = (now - 2*p_days, now - p_days]; each bucket picks the latest attempt per distinct question inside that bucket, excluding review / blind-review replays; returns per qtype and bucket: questions, correct, missed, last_attempt_at.\\r\\n33:- `home_lr_type_evidence(p_class_id, p_qtype, p_days)`: the qids behind the current bucket; both RPCs call one shared SQL selection function so numbers and evidence cannot disagree.\\r\\n34:\\r\\n35:**Sections adapter** over `section_history` (not the zero-filling `sectionHistory.ts:113` reader): totals from `total_questions` / `initial_score`, unknown dates stay unknown (excluded from recency), label \\"Sections\\" unless `mode` proves timing; blind-review delta shown as evidence only.\\r\\n36:\\r\\n37:**Section diagnosis**: top saved finding from `analysis_json` via `sectionAnalysis.ts`.\\r\\n38:\\r\\n39:**RC**: not a lens in this build (its readers are unbounded, `rcProgress.ts`); deferred with a bounded aggregate to build two.\\r\\n40:\\r\\n41:### 3. Insight engine (fixed rules, no AI text)\\r\\n42:Pure `pickInsights(signals)` in `home/insights.ts`, fixture-tested. Lenses: LR types, Sections, Section diagnosis. Floors: types �8 distinct questions in `current`; sections �1 in 21 days. Score = recency + actionability, normalized per lens; fixed tie order; one card per source. The chosen insight is frozen for the visit; new data shows a quiet \\"New results, refresh\\" control and never reorders under the pointer.\\r\\n43:\\r\\n44:### 4. Diagnosis-to-action (leads the main card)\\r\\n45:- Registry `home/actionRegistry.ts`: 4-8 entries `{ findingRule, launch body or route, reason }`, drafted by Claude from existing bootcamps, guided drills, Read Recall Colour and timed sections; Joshua reviews once before merge.\\r\\n46:- Card order: headline (what the evidence shows); the recommended action with its one-line reason; \\"Practice <type>\\" (`kind: \'filter\'` with that qtype); \\"Review these\\" (journal entries of that type, when any); \\"See the evidence\\". Unmapped finding: \\"See the evidence\\" replaces the recommendation.\\r\\n47:- **New-questions-only**: \\"Practice <type>\\" prefers unseen questions. Requires an additive `excludeAnswered?: boolean` on the filter launch body, applied BEFORE sampling where the bank is filtered (`Drill.tsx:565`), with the built queue persisted in the Run Record so resume replays it. Shortfall: \\"Only N new <type> questions left\\" with \\"Include ones you have answered\\". This ships in the same PR as the button (PR 2), never after.\\r\\n48:- Evidence drawer: exact addresses from the evidence RPC or section `questions_json`; counts, window, \\"updated <time>\\".\\r\\n49:\\r\\n50:### 5. Honest visuals\\r\\n51:- Bubbles: area proportional to missed (radius = k * sqrt(missed)), computed, never hardcoded; each labelled with type, accuracy, \\"missed of attempted\\"; two-size legend; keyboard-selectable, selecting highlights the list row. Fewer than 3 eligible types: labelled results instead of bubbles.\\r\\n52:- Labels never white on orange: bubble text is ink `#1f1b18` on the orange fill, or white only on the dark bubble; every text/fill pair verified �4.5:1 (�3:1 at 24px+) in light and dark themes.\\r\\n53:- The list below is the text equivalent of the bubbles (screen readers get the list; bubbles are `aria-hidden` with a summary label).\\r\\n54:- Copy: \\"Most misses in your recent LR practice\\", never \\"costing you points\\".\\r\\n55:- List: continuous bars, questions per type, change \\"pts vs previous 30 days\\" only when both buckets have �8 questions.\\r\\n56:\\r\\n57:### 6. Ask with evidence\\r\\n58:\\"Ask\\" on the main insight opens a draft in the existing inbox to Joshua, prefilled with the insight\'s evidence and a comment field. Nothing sends without the student\'s Send. Label \\"Ask\\" (glossary). Uses the existing message insert path unchanged; no new message columns in this build.\\r\\n59:\\r\\n60:### 7. Sure but wrong (gated)\\r\\n61:Claude runs a read-only audit before PR 4: share of last-30-day `attempts` with non-null `confidence`, per write path, and whether each path captures confidence before feedback. Ship only if �60% coverage for at least half of active students; else defer. Card: labelled 2x2 (1-2 unsure, 4-5 sure), coverage line, floor 12 rated first attempts, tap a cell for its questions.\\r\\n62:\\r\\n63:### 8. Quality bar (release requirement)\\r\\n64:Cache-first paint on return visits; layout-matched skeletons on first load; no spinners. Count-up once per local day; motion transform/opacity only; none under `prefers-reduced-motion`. WCAG 2.2 AA incl. 200% text, focus order, contrast on gradient and taupe. Accommodated timer used for any pace figure. Verification: a student test account at 390 / 900 / 1440 and docked widths, dark class, keyboard only, reduced motion. Measure Home first paint before and after PR 2.\\r\\n65:\\r\\n66:### 9. Build order (PRs)\\r\\n67:1. Layout + top panel + tiles + Book-a-session state on existing data, on top of #133 (closes #133).\\r\\n68:2. RPCs (with auth tests) + adapters + insight engine + honest visuals + list + evidence drawer + `excludeAnswered`.\\r\\n69:3. Registry (after Joshua\'s review) + diagnosis-to-action + Ask draft.\\r\\n70:4. Sure but wrong, only if the audit passes.\\r\\n71:Build two (separate plan): session brief; review then fresh check; Today shortcut; coach pinning with optional note; unified notifications (event identity, read semantics, reminders); RC lens; launcher Section tab reusing `rcSectionHref` (`rcSections.ts:131`) and `kind: \'section\'`.\\r\\n72:\\r\\n73:## Key decisions & tradeoffs\\r\\n74:- Diagnosis leads; coach notes never required.\\r\\n75:- Bubbles for character, list for precision and accessibility.\\r\\n76:- Frozen insight per visit.\\r\\n77:- Small human-reviewed registry, not inference.\\r\\n78:- Sure but wrong gated by audit, no new rating step.\\r\\n79:- Unified notifications deferred whole, so its identity and read-state design is done once, properly.\\r\\n80:\\r\\n81:## Risks / open questions\\r\\n82:- RPC cost on first paint.\\r\\n83:- Registry quality depends on one review.\\r\\n84:- Student test account needed.\\r\\n85:- Taupe/gradient contrast may force darker text than the mockup.\\r\\n86:\\r\\n87:## Out of scope\\r\\n88:Slim menu rail; notifications merge and bell popover; pinning; session brief; review loop; Today; RC lens; launcher tabs; goal score, streaks, PT score, month calendar; AI text; teacher view redesign; public landing page.\\r\\n"}'}]

'text': '{"status":"fulfilled","value":{"chunk_id":"7896b3","wall_time_seconds":1.0856346000000001,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1881,"output":"/**\\r\\n * Sat sections - reading `section_history` back out.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The table has been written since the first full section shipped and, until\\r\\n * now, read by exactly two things: the dashboard\'s last-five widget and the\\r\\n * practice-test scorer. No page ever showed a student the sections they had\\r\\n * sat. A section was a screen you got once, at the moment you finished, and\\r\\n * never again.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * This is the reader. A sat section becomes an object with a history: a score,\\r\\n * a blind review or the honest absence of one, timing, and - for rows written\\r\\n * after `questions_json` started being persisted - the question-by-question\\r\\n * record behind the totals.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Rows written before that carry `questions_json: []`. They are not broken and\\r\\n * they are not empty sections; the detail simply was not kept. {@link hasDetail}\\r\\n * is the distinction, so the UI can say so rather than render a blank table.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport type { SectionQuestionStat } from \'@/lib/sectionHistorySave\';\\r\\nimport type { SectionAnalysis } from \'@/lib/sectionAnalysis\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface SectionHistoryRow {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  pt: number;\\r\\n  section: number;\\r\\n  mode: string | null;\\r\\n  initialScore: number;\\r\\n  initialTotal: number;\\r\\n  initialPercent: number;\\r\\n  /** null when no blind review was sat. Never 0 - that would be a review scored. */\\r\\n  brScore: number | null;\\r\\n  brPercent: number | null;\\r\\n  brDelta: number | null;\\r\\n  brUsed: boolean;\\r\\n  totalTimeMs: number;\\r\\n  avgTimeMs: number;\\r\\n  unansweredCount: number;\\r\\n  byQtype: Record<string, { correct: number; total: number }>;\\r\\n  byDifficulty: Record<string, { correct: number; total: number }>;\\r\\n  /** Empty for sections sat before the per-question record was persisted. */\\r\\n  questions: SectionQuestionStat[];\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * The diagnosis written at completion. Null for sittings from before the\\r\\n   * engine existed, which must render as \\"no report\\" rather than as an empty\\r\\n   * one - an empty report reads as \\"nothing was wrong\\".\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  analysis: SectionAnalysis | null;\\r\\n  completedAt: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Did this row keep its question-by-question record? */\\r\\nexport const hasDetail = (row: SectionHistoryRow): boolean => row.questions.length > 0;\\r\\n\\r\\n/** `PT105.S2`, the label 7Sage uses and the one that reads fastest. */\\r\\nexport const sectionLabel = (row: SectionHistoryRow): string =>\\r\\n  `PT${row.pt}.S${row.section}`;\\r\\n\\r\\nconst asTally = (v: unknown): Record<string, { correct: number; total: number }> =>\\r\\n  v && typeof v === \'object\' && !Array.isArray(v)\\r\\n    ? (v as Record<string, { correct: number; total: number }>)\\r\\n    : {};\\r\\n\\r\\nconst asQuestions = (v: unknown): SectionQuestionStat[] =>\\r\\n  Array.isArray(v) ? (v as SectionQuestionStat[]) : [];\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Every section this student has sat, newest first.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Filtered on `user_id` rather than `class_id`: the row carries both, and the\\r\\n * question \\"which sections have I sat\\" is about the person, not the class they\\r\\n * happened to be in at the time.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function fetchSectionHistory(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n): Promise<SectionHistoryRow[]> {\\r\\n  return fetchSectionHistoryBy(\'user_id\', userId);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The same list, addressed by class rather than by user.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The teacher workspace knows a student by their class_id - it is what the\\r\\n * roster, the classroom items and every assignment are keyed on - and has no\\r\\n * reason to hold their auth user id. Reading these rows at all depends on the\\r\\n * admin SELECT policy; without it this returns an empty list rather than an\\r\\n * error, which is why the teacher surface must never render an empty result\\r\\n * as \\"this student has done nothing\\".\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function fetchSectionHistoryByClass(\\r\\n  classId: string,\\r\\n): Promise<SectionHistoryRow[]> {\\r\\n  return fetchSectionHistoryBy(\'class_id\', classId);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function fetchSectionHistoryBy(\\r\\n  column: \'user_id\' | \'class_id\',\\r\\n  value: string,\\r\\n): Promise<SectionHistoryRow[]> {\\r\\n  // `*` rather than a column list, because the generated types are regenerated\\r\\n  // from the deployed schema and reject `analysis_json` by name until the\\r\\n  // migration that adds it has been applied. A star select is valid against\\r\\n  // both, returns the column once it exists, and simply omits it before then -\\r\\n  // which is exactly what the `?? null` below is for.\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\'section_history\')\\r\\n    .select(\'*\')\\r\\n    .eq(column, value)\\r\\n    .order(\'completed_at\', { ascending: false });\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n\\r\\n  return ((data ?? []) as unknown as Array<Record<string, any>>).map((r) => ({\\r\\n    id: r.id,\\r\\n    pt: r.pt,\\r\\n    section: r.section,\\r\\n    mode: r.mode,\\r\\n    initialScore: r.initial_score ?? 0,\\r\\n    initialTotal: r.initial_total ?? 0,\\r\\n    initialPercent: r.initial_percent ?? 0,\\r\\n    // `br_used` is the authority, not the presence of a number. A row from\\r\\n    // before the empty-array fix can carry a br_score for a review that was\\r\\n    // never sat; without this guard those rows would report it as real.\\r\\n    brScore: r.br_used ? r.br_score : null,\\r\\n    brPercent: r.br_used ? r.br_percent : null,\\r\\n    brDelta: r.br_used ? r.br_delta : null,\\r\\n    brUsed: Boolean(r.br_used),\\r\\n    totalTimeMs: r.total_time_ms ?? 0,\\r\\n    avgTimeMs: r.avg_time_ms ?? 0,\\r\\n    unansweredCount: r.unanswered_count ?? 0,\\r\\n    byQtype: asTally(r.by_qtype_json),\\r\\n    byDifficulty: asTally(r.by_difficulty_json),\\r\\n    questions: asQuestions(r.questions_json),\\r\\n    analysis: (r.analysis_json as SectionAnalysis) ?? null,\\r\\n    completedAt: r.completed_at ?? r.created_at ?? new Date().toISOString(),\\r\\n  }));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface SectionSummary {\\r\\n  sections: number;\\r\\n  /** Highest percent scored. null when nothing has been sat. */\\r\\n  bestPercent: number | null;\\r\\n  bestLabel: string | null;\\r\\n  averagePercent: number | null;\\r\\n  totalTimeMs: number;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Best and average across a set of sections, 7Sage\'s two headline tiles.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Averages the percent rather than the raw score, because sections are not all\\r\\n * the same length and a mean of raw scores across a 24 and a 26 is a number\\r\\n * that means nothing.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function summarizeSections(rows: SectionHistoryRow[]): SectionSummary {\\r\\n  if (rows.length === 0) {\\r\\n    return {\\r\\n      sections: 0,\\r\\n      bestPercent: null,\\r\\n      bestLabel: null,\\r\\n      averagePercent: null,\\r\\n      totalTimeMs: 0,\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const best = rows.reduce((a, b) => (b.initialPercent > a.initialPercent ? b : a));\\r\\n  const sum = rows.reduce((n, r) => n + r.initialPercent, 0);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    sections: rows.length,\\r\\n    bestPercent: best.initialPercent,\\r\\n    bestLabel: sectionLabel(best),\\r\\n    averagePercent: Math.round(sum / rows.length),\\r\\n    totalTimeMs: rows.reduce((n, r) => n + r.totalTimeMs, 0),\\r\\nrg: src/lib/sectionCompletion.ts: The system cannot find the file specified. ## assistant

Reviewed the saved **“Act 5, Round 1 revision”**. Unified notifications remain excluded. These issues still need correction:

1. **P1 — The section adapter trusts a known bad legacy denominator.** [PLAN.md:35](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:35) derives totals from `total_questions`, but [sectionHistorySave.ts:324](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/lib/sectionHistorySave.ts:324) documents historical rows carrying zero there while `initial_total` contains the real total. Valid work can become `19/0` or disappear as insufficient.
   **Fix:** Prefer valid `initial_total`, fall back to positive `total_questions`, preserve unknown denominators, and require `br_used` before showing blind-review results.

2. **P1 — Shared SQL logic does not guarantee matching evidence snapshots.** [PLAN.md:32](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:32) uses `now()` for each RPC, while [line 42](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:42) freezes the insight for the visit. Opening evidence later can include a new attempt, replace the selected attempt for a question, or exclude one crossing the window boundary.
   **Fix:** Return a snapshot cutoff with the aggregate and pass it to evidence queries, retaining selected attempt IDs where necessary.

3. **P1 — Fresh-only launches can resume an unrestricted queue.** [PLAN.md:47](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:47) adds `excludeAnswered`, but the existing resume fingerprint includes only the current filter fields ([drillResumeSession.ts:93](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/lib/drillResumeSession.ts:93)). Identical filters with different freshness settings therefore share a resume record unless explicitly changed.
   **Fix:** Include freshness policy in the resume identity, preserve existing identities for unrestricted links, and test launching fresh-only after an unfinished unrestricted run.

4. **P1 — “Timed section” still cannot start a timed section.** [PLAN.md:22](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:22) deliberately retains #133’s finder action while deferring section launching to build two. On `feat/home-v2-phase1`, `ClassroomHomeView.tsx:287` only calls `open()`; the finder returns individual questions/passages. This leaves a promised primary action without its advertised destination.
   **Fix:** Route the button to an existing working section-selection surface now; defer the new launcher tab independently.

5. **P1 — Keyboard-selectable bubbles cannot also be hidden from assistive technology.** [PLAN.md:51](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:51) makes bubbles interactive, while [line 53](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:53) applies `aria-hidden`. A summary label does not restore hidden controls, leaving keyboard focus on elements screen readers cannot identify.
   **Fix:** Either expose labelled bubble controls with selection state, or make bubbles entirely noninteractive and provide selection through accessible list controls.

6. **P2 — Perfect accuracy produces invisible bubbles.** [PLAN.md:51](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:51) specifies `radius = k * sqrt(missed)` but only falls back when fewer than three types qualify. Three sufficiently practised types with zero misses qualify yet produce zero-radius circles; mixed results also lose labels for perfect types.
   **Fix:** Define an all-zero-misses result state and represent zero-miss types in the list without inventing a minimum bubble area.

7. **P2 — Confidence level 3 falls outside the promised matrix.** [PLAN.md:61](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:61) counts non-null confidence for coverage and requires 12 rated attempts, but its matrix includes only ratings 1–2 and 4–5. Twelve neutral ratings can satisfy the floor while populating no cells, and displayed totals can disagree with the coverage line.
   **Fix:** Explicitly exclude and disclose neutral ratings, calculating the matrix’s eligibility floor from attempts actually represented in its cells.

No files modified; findings are based on source inspection, not deployed-data verification.

## assistant

One material issue remains:

**P1 — The headline practice count has no active writer in the inspected code.** [PLAN.md:22](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/brainstorms/2026-09-23-classroom-home-PLAN.md:22) uses `daily_stats.questions_answered`, but both the current checkout and `feat/home-v2-phase1` only read that counter. `increment_daily_stats` exists as a migration-defined RPC with no application caller; the drill persistence path writes `attempts` and returns ([Drill.tsx:1482](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/src/pages/Drill.tsx:1482)). Consequently, completed practice can leave the prominent weekly number stale or zero. No deployed-only writer was verified.

**Fix:** Derive the count from persisted attempts using explicit local-day boundaries, replay exclusions, and an LR coverage label; verify that completing practice increments it.

The seven previous fixes are reflected in the revision. No files modified.

VERDICT: REVISE
